# PR bigint_bigdecimal_coerce (2026-06-04): flipped 9 drr POJO OUT of
# CODEGEN_BODY_GAP (1230 -> 1221); total 3794 -> 3785, output parity
# 66.85% -> 66.93%. Root cause: a `number` literal exceeding `long`
# (9999999999999999999999999 -> Java BigInteger), passed as a function-call argument
# INSIDE an extract/map lambda where the callee parameter expects number (-> BigDecimal).
# LiteralHandler renders the literal as a typeless `new BigInteger("...")`, so the fork
# inlined it raw as the evaluate arg -- a non-compiling BigInteger-where-BigDecimal type
# mismatch. Golden hoists the value into a `final BigInteger bigInteger = new
# BigInteger("...");` local and passes the null-guarded conversion
# (bigInteger == null ? null : new BigDecimal(bigInteger)), lifting the lambda to BLOCK

# PR neq_default_cardinality (2026-06-03): flipped 9 drr FUNCTION OUT of

# 65.20% -> 65.28%. Root cause: ComparisonHandler emitted CardinalityOperator.All

# PR #107 (2026-06-02): flipped 41 drr POJO data types OUT of CODEGEN_BODY_GAP
# (1424 -> 1383; total 4790 -> 4749) via choice-vs-data builder-interface setter
# param naming. ModelObjectGenerator.interfaceParamName prefixed the interface
# setter parameter with `_` whenever the property name started uppercase — a proxy
# for choice-option-ness that holds for CDM (choice options PascalCase, data attrs
# camelCase) but mis-fires on the ISO-UPI AnnaDsb* DRR *data* types, whose
# attributes (SEAF, GRIN, AGRI, AdditionalSubProduct, …) legitimately start
# uppercase. Upstream actually prefixes `_` to avoid the param identifier colliding
# with a same-named type: iff the property name equals its value-type simple name.
# That covers `choice` options named after their type (setCash(Cash _Cash)), choice
# options inherited by a `type extends choice` (BasketConstituent -> setAsset(Asset
# _Asset)), and meta-wrapped options against the UNWRAPPED value type
# (setInterestRateIndex(FieldWithMetaInterestRateIndex _InterestRateIndex)); the
# AnnaDsb data attrs collide with nothing (SEAF != AnnaDsbEmpty) so stay bare.
# interfaceParamName now returns "_"+name iff name == itemTypeName(prop) or ==
# getMetaValueType().getSimpleName(), validated against 3947 golden setters. The 41
# are the AnnaDsb* data POJOs whose only residual diff was the spurious `_`; the 2
# list-bearing AnnaDsb types (AnnaDsbDerived / AnnaDsbUnderlyingRecord) carry a
# separate PascalCase list-field-case bug and stay waivered. drr dump-now-matching
# baseline was 0 pre-fix (all 41 attributable, not stale-waiver cleanup); cdm/iso/

# first cut keyed on RChoice build-path alone regressed 4 cdm POJO (BasketConstituent
# inherits choice options and keeps `_`) — caught by the full matrix, hence the
# collision rule. M7B_PAUSED unchanged.

# PR #231 reserved_keyword_escape (2026-06-19): flipped 11 POJO OUT of
# CODEGEN_DRIFT (36 -> 25); total 1581 -> 1570. Root cause: a Rosetta attribute
# literally named with a Java reserved word (new / short / long / return / volatile)
# was emitted as a BARE keyword field/param/accessor identifier (a syntax error)
# where golden escapes it (`_new` field + body refs, `__new` impl setter param,
# `_new` interface setter param; @RosettaAttribute/@RuneAttribute/newSubPath strings
# stay raw). Fix mirrors upstream's scope escape (SourceVersion.isName →
# JavaClassScope.escapeName "_"+name) via JavaNamingUtil.escapeJavaKeyword applied at
# ModelObjectGenerator.fieldName + interfaceParamName + the ModelObjectBoilerplate
# equals/hashCode/toString field refs. Carriers: 8 iso TradeReport*Choice (`new`) +
# fpml CreditLimitUtilizationPosition (`short`/`long`) + fpml ReturnLeg (`return`) +
# fpml CoalStandardQuality (`volatile`, byte-oracle-surfaced, census-missed). Green-safe
# by construction (escape is a no-op for valid identifiers; a bare keyword never
# compiled, so every carrier was already waivered). byte-oracle 11 EXACT (stash-baseline
# confirmed mine; the 12 other waivered-but-now-matching drr Rule POJOs are PRE-EXISTING
# stale matches left waivered due to the documented drr rule-family meta-wrapper
# non-determinism — ReferenceWithMeta<->FieldWithMeta — which the waiver safely tolerates).


# === PERMANENT (frozen-at-clone; no fix without regenerating goldens) ===

# THE CDM-6.20.6 UPSTREAM BUG (user-directed note, PR #405 — evidence session-verified
# 2026-07-15/16): this golden is an artifact of an upstream MODEL/BUILD DEFECT, not a
# fork gap. Evidence: (1) it exists ONLY in the cdm-6.20.6 cell — absent from cdm5 and
# every other cell; (2) it is an ORPHAN — zero references to it anywhere in the
# 34,686-file golden tree; (3) it wraps java.lang.Void (`ReferenceWithMeta<Void>`),
# upstream's Java mapping for the bottom/`nothing` type — i.e. a TYPE-RESOLUTION
# FAILURE product; no `Void`/`Nothing`-named type exists in any .rosetta source;
# (4) it sits in the shared BASIC-type wrapper package (beside ReferenceWithMetaDate/
# ReferenceWithMetaString), meaning the triggering `[metadata reference]` attribute's
# type resolved to NOTHING at upstream golden-build time rather than to any declared
# type. Upstream's MetaFieldGenerator manufactures one wrapper per distinct RESOLVED
# type among [metadata reference] attributes; when one attribute's resolution bottomed
# out it silently minted a Void wrapper instead of failing the build. The fork resolves
# the same model closure cleanly (zero generation errors, all 86 other cdm6 wrappers
# byte-identical) and correctly emits nothing for an unresolvable reference — hence the
# permanent missing-output.
# DRAFT FINOS ISSUE (file when the repo goes public): "cdm 6.20.6 generated artifact
# ReferenceWithMetaVoid.java appears to be a code-generation artifact over an
# unresolved [metadata reference] attribute type: it wraps java.lang.Void (the
# rune-dsl mapping for an unresolvable/nothing type), is referenced by no other
# generated file, and has no corresponding Void/Nothing model type. Suggest the
# generator fail loudly on an unresolved reference type rather than emitting a
# Void wrapper, and that the triggering attribute in CDM 6.20.6 be identified and
# fixed."
# v3.1 phase C, C0 item 5 — RE-KEYED FROM cdm/6.20.6 TO THE CLASS (2026-08-15).
# The 25-cell band showed this identical missing output in TWELVE cells, not one:
# cdm 6.20.2 / 6.20.3 / 6.20.4 / 6.20.5 / 6.20.6 / 6.21.0 / 6.22.0 / 6.23.0 and the
# four DRR 7.x cells that inherit the CDM 6 line (drr 7.0.0 / 7.1.0 / 7.2.0 / 7.3.0),
# exactly one per cell, every one verified to be this same class. The cause is
# upstream's and cell-independent (it wraps java.lang.Void; zero golden references;
# no model type), so a per-cell key was an artifact of the 5-cell corpus and would
# have needed a new line for every future CDM 6 cell. '*/*/KIND' waives one PATH
# across the band; the loader requires both wildcards together and validates the KIND
# exactly as before. Root cause + the draft FINOS issue are unchanged, above.
*/*/METAFIELD:com/rosetta/model/metafields/ReferenceWithMetaVoid.java

# FINOS-ISSUE RIDER #7 (PR #632, v3.2 seat 11 M1 / D50 — file with the riders above when
# the repo goes public): "rune-dsl 9.83.0 writes two library references as raw literals
# outside its import-aware first-claim law, so a data type named after them yields
# NON-COMPILING Java: the list setter's `.collect(Collectors.toCollection(()->new
# ArrayList<>()))` beside a data type named ArrayList (javac: cannot use '<>' with
# non-generic class — while every OTHER ArrayList reference in the same file is written
# java.util.ArrayList), and the only-exists validator's `.filter(Map.Entry::getValue)` /
# `.map(Map.Entry::getKey)` beside a data type named Map (javac: cannot find symbol
# variable Entry). Reproduced from the released rosetta-maven-plugin:9.83.0 (the fork's
# oracle-pinned goldens holdout-goldens/type-named-util/.../ArrayList.java, UtilRefs.java
# and validation/exists/MapOnlyExistsValidator.java); the fork reproduces the bytes and
# documents the three files in HoldOutCompileGateTest.EXPECTED_NON_COMPILING_UPSTREAM_BUG.
# Suggest both literals go through the import-aware type reference."
#
# FINOS-ISSUE RIDER #8 (PR #632, v3.2 seat 11 M1 / D50 — file with the riders above when
# the repo goes public): "rune-dsl 9.83.0 emits NON-COMPILING Java for a data type named
# RosettaModelObject: the POJO's nested builder is declared `interface
# RosettaModelObjectBuilder extends RosettaModelObject, RosettaModelObjectBuilder` under
# `import com.rosetta.model.lib.RosettaModelObjectBuilder` — inside the class the nested
# interface's OWN name shadows the import, so javac reports cyclic inheritance, and the
# type's three validators plus the two validators of any sibling holding it as an
# attribute cascade (cannot find symbol). Reproduced from the released
# rosetta-maven-plugin:9.83.0 (the fork's oracle-pinned golden
# holdout-goldens/type-named-rosetta/.../RosettaModelObject.java); the fork reproduces the
# bytes and documents the file and its five cascades in
# HoldOutCompileGateTest.EXPECTED_NON_COMPILING_UPSTREAM_BUG. Suggest the nested builder's
# library super-interface be written fully qualified when the nested name shadows it."
#
# HEALED AT PR #413 — the 4 package-info order waivers (added PR #405, wave A) are
# RETIRED. The #405 analysis stands: each namespace's DESCRIPTION ORDER is an upstream
# BUILD-ORDER artifact (vendored-source-proven at #413: upstream JavaPackageInfoGenerator
# collects namespace→definition into a Guava LinkedHashMultimap [insertion-ordered,
# (key,value)-deduping] fed from resourceSet.getResources() in LOAD order =
# StandaloneBuilder → PathTraverser → File.listFiles() = the build machine's readdir
# order — unordered per javadoc; same model text, four different historical
# enumerations; the reproducible-builds defect to report upstream alongside the
# ReferenceWithMetaVoid issue above when the repo goes public). The heal: the golden
# orders were DECODED from bytes via description-text fingerprints (trailing
# space/period deltas) and are REPLAYED by per-cell input-order pins in the D11
# loader — D11CorpusRegressionTest.GOLDEN_INPUT_ORDER_PINS (the versionStamp
# precedent: recorded per-cell build-input truth as loader data, never a generator
# hack; fail-loud on corpus drift). All 149 package-info files byte-identical since.
